```json
{
  "type": "queue-operation",
  "operation": "enqueue",
  "timestamp": "2026-05-24T04:19:50.940Z",
  "sessionId": "824f3111-42c3-4ec6-b193-944b13769b09",
  "content": "[审稿报告]\nOverall: 3.5 - Borderline Conference (推荐 Findings)\n\n# Round 23 审稿报告\n\n**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ARR)  \n**格式**: Long Paper, 8-page main text limit  \n**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿)  \n**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人（R1: 方法严谨性/理论完整性; R2: 新颖性/完整性/相关工作）  \n**轮次**: 1 轮（交叉回应后达成共识，无需额外轮次）\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文系统分析 LLM 标注误差在因果推断中的传播机制，覆盖 7 种 DAG 拓扑 × K 类别。核心贡献包括：(1) K≥3 下标注误差引发质性新现象（exposure 拓扑 83.6% sign reversal vs 其他拓扑 0-2.1%），建立跨拓扑的 bias taxonomy；(2) Annotation Sensitivity Value (ASV) 诊断工具，量化混淆矩阵到 sign flip 的最小扰动；(3) FWL futility result 证明 4 种 covariate 拓扑下矩阵校正在 OLS 中无效，以及 topology-aware correction guidance。理论框架建立在 non-differential misclassification + linear SEM + proportional effects 三个核心假设之上。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n**S1. 系统性覆盖的独立价值。** 首次在统一框架下比较 7 种 DAG 拓扑 × K 类别的标注误差传播行为。K≥3 引入的 structural dichotomy 是质性新现象（而非 K=2 的量的推广）：exposure 拓扑在 K≥3 下从安全变为高危（83.6% sign reversal），confounding/mediation 在 K≥3 下也出现非零 violation rate（13.7% under realistic accuracy）。这不是 Brenner (1993) 的直接推广。\n\n**S2. 严格的理论层次。** K=2 exact proof (Theorem 1) → K=3 symmetric exact (Theorem 2) → general K≥3 conjecture with extensive numerical verification (545,945 adversarial curves)。理论推导在适用范围内是正确和完整的。\n\n**S3. FWL futility result 的实践价值。** 虽然证明技巧简短（FWL 定理的直接推论），但 observation 本身对 NLP 实践者有重要警示价值：使用 confusion matrix 校正标注再跑回归在 4 种 covariate 拓扑下 provably useless。这是一个 counterintuitive negative result。（两位 Reviewer 对其 novelty 有分歧——见 Divergence D1。）\n\n**S4. 真实 LLM 混淆矩阵的验证。** Section 4.4 使用 23 个真实 LLM 配置的混淆矩阵验证 bias ranking 和 sign-flip predictions；Appendix B.18 Political Ad case study、B.19-B.20 semi-real case study 提供超越纯模拟的实证成分。\n\n**S5. 数值验证覆盖面广。** MC 模拟 MAPE 1.6%，100,000 Dirichlet-sampled matrices，adversarial monotonicity 验证，bootstrap CI 收敛分析。在计算数学标准下，这是 strong evidence。\n\n**S6. 实践工具完整。** Algorithm 1 将理论发现转化为决策流程（risk assessment → correction routing → FWL check），对 practitioner 有直接指导价值。\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses\n\n### Must-Fix（两位 Reviewer 共识）\n\n**MF-1 [Critical | R1-N3 + R2-N2 | 共识 AGREE]: Conjecture 1 未证明，削弱 K≥3 ASV 框架的理论可靠性。**\n\nConjecture 1（general K≥3 approximate envelope monotonicity）是 ASV 框架的理论基石。依赖链：Conjecture 1 → ASV vulnerability ranking 有序性 → Algorithm 1 Step 1 risk level 判断 → correction routing 正确性。若 conjecture 存在反例，Algorithm 1 可能给出错误建议。\n\n数值验证虽然广泛（545,945 adversarial curves, max step deviation < 0.04），但存在两个隐忧：(a) Appendix B.12 Table 11 显示 K=7 worst-case magnitude deviation 0.060，比 K=3 的 0.003 大一个数量级——approximate monotonicity 质量随 K 增大恶化，在 HumAID K=10 下可靠性存疑；(b) adversarial search 的 4 种结构族基于直觉选择，是否覆盖 worst case 不清楚。\n\n论文应讨论：如果 approximate monotonicity 偶尔被违反（ε < 0.017），Algorithm 1 的 robustness 如何？小幅违反是否导致 correction routing 质变？\n\n**MF-2 [Critical | R1-N2 + R2-W1→升级 | 共识 AGREE]: Non-differential misclassification 假设与论文自身 stress test 结果构成内部一致性矛盾。**\n\n论文全部理论结果依赖 non-differential misclassification（标注误差独立于 outcome），但论文自己的实证检验显示该假设在目标场景中被系统性违反：\n- Appendix B.6 Table 8-9: 13.3% baseline violation rate\n- 14/16 K≥3 exposure configurations 在 α=0.01 下出现 structured departures（正文 p.4）\n- Appendix B.26 Y-stratified permutation test 在 VAST 数据上 13.3% violation rate\n\nR2 补充 violation 主要是 content-dependent（topic/sentiment driven）而非纯粹 outcome-dependent，在控制 topic 后可能不违反假设。但这个论点在论文中未被充分发展。当理论框架的核心假设在目标应用中被系统性违反时，理论结果的实际指导价值需要更诚实的讨论。\n\n**MF-3 [Critical/Should-Fix | R1-N1 + R2 partially agree | 共识 PARTIALLY AGREE]: Theorem 1 的 proportional effects 假设在 NLP 中几乎不可验证。**\n\nTheorem 1 要求所有类别的 treatment effect 按同一比例缩放。论文的数值验证使用满足该假设的 DGP，non-proportional sparse DGP 下 24.2% 违反率被正文忽略。R2 认为 R1 对假设不合理性的判断\"稍过强\"（ordinal 单调效应场景下是合理近似），但双方共识：论文缺少\"NLP 中何时/何种任务下 proportional effects 合理\"的实质讨论。\n\n---\n\n### Should-Fix（两位 Reviewer 共识或多数同意）\n\n**SF-1 [R1-W1 + R2-W2 | 共识 AGREE]: Linear SEM 假设严重限制 practical recommendations 的适用范围。**\n\nAppendix B.7 nonlinear DGP 测试显示 sign-flip rate 在 structured violation 下仍为 0%（部分 robustness），但 matrix-based correction 在 logistic regression 下 bias reduction ≈ 0%，FWL invariance 在非线性模型下形式上不再成立。论文的 practical recommendations 对非线性模型（matching, doubly-robust, deep learning）几乎完全不适用。这对 EMNLP 读者群体（更多使用非线性模型）构成重大适用性限制。\n\n**SF-2 [R1-W4 + R2-N3 | 共识 AGREE]: 端到端真实因果验证缺失。**\n\n论文的 \"empirical validation\" 本质是 theory-MC consistency check + retrospective analysis（已知 ground truth 或 simulated outcomes）。缺少在 researcher 不知 ground truth 的真实因果推断场景中展示框架 decision-making value 的验证。理想验证：对一个有 gold-standard causal effect 的 NLP 任务，展示 Algorithm 1 routing 后的 corrected estimate 比 naive estimate 更接近 ground truth。\n\n两位 Reviewer 均认为获取同时有 gold labels 和已知因果效应的真实数据很困难，因此定级 should-fix 而非 must-fix。\n\n**SF-3 [R2-W3 | R1 AGREE]: 相关工作缺口。**\n- 缺少与 Cinelli & Hazlett (2020) partial R² sensitivity analysis 的方法论对比\n- 缺少 regression calibration (Carroll et al. 2006) 在 categorical misclassification 下的不适用性讨论\n- PPI++ 理论 guarantees (Angelopoulos et al. 2023) 值得在 Related Work 中讨论（R1 认为非重大遗漏）\n\n**SF-4 [R1-W3 | R2 AGREE]: C(δ) 线性插值参数化的局限性。**\n\nC(δ) = (1-δ)I + δC₀ 假设误分类模式在质量变化时保持结构不变，但 LLM 在不同 prompt/temperature 下混淆矩阵结构可能定性改变。ASV 的 \"minimum perturbation to sign flip\" 解释可能不准确。R2 认为这是 sensitivity analysis 通用问题（E-values 类似），非本文特有缺陷。应 acknowledge 但不必改变方法。\n\n**SF-5 [R1-Q1 + R2 AGREE]: B_OV 间接可观测性问题。**\n\nProposition 1(a) 中 B_OV = Cov(g(A), f(A)) / Var(f(A)) 涉及真实 A 而非 observed A*。Section 3.3 通过 bootstrap (A* → C⁻¹ → A) 间接估计，但 estimation error propagation 未讨论。这是 ASV 从理论到实用的潜在瓶颈。\n\n---\n\n### Minor Issues\n\n**m1**: Figure 2 轴标签字号偏小（~7pt），视觉审查和 R2 均指出。R1 认为密度在 8 页限制下合理。\n\n**m2**: Section 3.1 notation 密度高（plim/FWL 对 NLP 读者不友好），R2 建议 notation table。\n\n**m3**: DAG misspecification under measurement error 应在 limitations 讨论。\n\n**m4**: AI assistance in proof development 未在 Section D 明确披露（ACL policy 要求充分描述）。\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus\n\n以下问题两位 Reviewer 达成完全或实质性共识：\n\n| 编号 | 问题 | 共识类型 | 严重性 |\n|------|------|----------|--------|\n| MF-1 | Conjecture 1 未证明 | [AGREE] 双方完全一致 | Critical |\n| MF-2 | Non-differential 假设内部矛盾 | [AGREE] 双方完全一致 | Critical |\n| MF-3 | Proportional effects 实践讨论缺失 | [PARTIALLY AGREE] | Critical/Should-Fix |\n| SF-1 | Linear SEM 限制 | [AGREE] 双方完全一致 | Should-Fix |\n| SF-2 | 端到端真实验证缺失 | [AGREE] 双方完全一致 | Should-Fix |\n| SF-3 | 相关工作缺口 | [AGREE] | Should-Fix |\n| SF-4 | C(δ) 参数化局限 | [AGREE] | Should-Fix |\n| SF-5 | B_OV 可观测性 | [AGREE] | Should-Fix |\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Divergence\n\n**D1 [FWL futility result novelty]: R1 认为 novelty 不足（FWL 定理直接推论，3-4 步证明），R2 认为 observation 的实践价值大于证明技巧的深度。**\n\n共识落点：R2 同意 R1 的 framing 建议——论文应将此定位为 \"a practically important observation that follows from FWL\"，而非暗示 deep theoretical contribution。贡献列表中与 bias taxonomy 和 ASV 并列可能给人过高 novelty 印象。\n\n**D2 [Theorem 2 symmetric assumption]: R1 认为 should-fix（VAST d_rel=0.30-0.38 远非对称，Remark 4 与正文脱节），R2 rebut（Theorem 2 定位为 theoretical stepping stone，论文处理得当）。**\n\n共识落点：Theorem 2 symmetric 假设本身不是 must-fix，但正文不应暗示 Theorem 2 直接适用于实际 K=3 场景。真正问题仍是 Conjecture 1。\n\n**D3 [Abstract \"annotation quality is the wrong thing to worry about\"]: R2 认为 overclaiming，R1 rebut（这是合理的 framing statement，正文内容支撑）。**\n\n共识落点：R1 论证更有说服力——Table 1 确实说明 aggregate accuracy 不是正确 metric，counterintuitive hook 是合理写作策略。保留。\n\n**D4 [核心贡献的增量性]: R2 强调技术手段标准、ASV 为 one-parameter sweep，R1 强调 K≥3 引入质性新现象赋予独立价值。**\n\n共识落点：双方在结论上相近——贡献 1 的核心价值在 K≥3 质性新现象而非量的推广；贡献 2-3 方法论深度有限。定性为 \"solid systematic contribution with novel qualitative insights\"。\n\n---\n\n## Questions for Authors\n\n**Q1**: Proposition 1(a) 中 B_OV 的估计依赖通过 C⁻¹ 从 A* 反推 A 的分布。这个间接估计步骤的 error propagation 如何？在 gold label budget 极少（50-100 samples）时 ASV 是否仍可靠？\n\n**Q2**: 如果 Conjecture 1 的 approximate monotonicity 在实践中偶尔被违反（ε < 0.017），Algorithm 1 的 correction routing 是否会发生质变——即推荐错误的 correction method？\n\n**Q3**: 论文能否提供一个 NLP 任务的具体例子，说明在什么条件下 proportional effects 假设是合理的近似？（如 ordinal sentiment classification）\n\n**Q4**: Non-differential misclassification violation 中，content-dependent 和 outcome-dependent 的区别是否可以被利用——即在控制 topic 后，论文的理论结果在条件 non-differential 意义下是否仍然成立？\n\n**Q5**: Section D 的 AI assistance 披露是否覆盖了定理证明过程？ACL 2025/2026 policy 要求 \"describe the use in sufficient detail\"。\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n**必须引用**:\n- Cinelli & Hazlett (2020) — partial R² sensitivity analysis，与 ASV 方法论对比（Section 2 已引用但未对比）\n- Regression calibration (Carroll et al. 2006) — 即使不适用于 categorical case 也应说明原因\n\n**建议引用（R22 遗留）**:\n- Mahajan (2006) — polytomous misclassification 非参数识别\n- Hu (2008) — 非线性模型下的误分类处理\n- Cornfield et al. (1959) — sensitivity analysis 方法论源头\n- Törnberg (2024) — LLM 注释质量实证\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n未请求外部审稿。\n\n---\n\n## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）\n\n**状态**: ✅ 已完成  \n**结果**: 0 critical, 1 warning, 6 info\n\n| 严重性 | 位置 | 发现 |\n|--------|------|------|\n| ⚠️ warning | p.23 | 附录单页堆叠 4 个 display items（Figure 5 + Figure 6 + Table 5 + Figure 7），版面极度拥挤 |\n| ℹ️ info | p.1-2 | 连续 2 页纯文本，无 display item。ACL 格式下属常规 |\n| ℹ️ info | p.5 | Figure 2 轴标签和拓扑名称字号偏小（~7pt） |\n| ℹ️ info | p.6 | 纯文本页（定理密集），属理论推导例外 |\n| ℹ️ info | p.8 | Figure 4 尺寸较小，适当放大可增强视觉冲击力 |\n| ℹ️ info | p.28-29 | Tables 12-13 列数多、字号小、数字密度高 |\n| ℹ️ info | p.35 | Algorithm 1 占 60% 页面，底部 40% 空白 |\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**评分: 3.5 — Borderline Conference（推荐 Findings）**\n\n**置信度: High（两位 Reviewer 独立给出相同评分，关键问题强共识）**\n\n本文在系统性和覆盖面上做出了扎实贡献——首次在统一框架下揭示 K≥3 标注误差传播的质性新现象（structural dichotomy），FWL futility result 对 NLP 实践者有直接警示价值。理论框架在 K=2 和 K=3 symmetric 范围内是完整的。\n\n阻碍进入主会议的核心障碍：\n\n1. **理论完整性缺口**：最重要的 general K≥3 case 停留在 conjecture 而非 theorem，ASV 框架的核心性质缺乏证明保障。K=7 worst-case deviation 的增长趋势加剧了这一担忧。\n\n2. **假设-实证矛盾**：论文理论依赖的 non-differential misclassification 假设在论文自己的 stress test 中被系统性违反（13.3% violation rate），形成内部一致性问题。若不能在条件 non-differential 或 robustness 层面化解这个矛盾，理论结果的实际指导价值存疑。\n\n3. **实践适用范围窄**：Linear SEM + proportional effects + non-differential 三个假设叠加，使得框架的直接适用场景在 NLP 中相当有限。Correction recommendations 对非线性模型几乎不适用。\n\n4. **方法论深度有限**：ASV 本质是 one-parameter sweep，Algorithm 1 更接近 practical guideline 而非 algorithmic contribution。贡献定位应为 \"solid systematic contribution with novel qualitative insights\" 而非 methodological breakthrough。\n\n**Findings 推荐理由**：覆盖度贡献真实（7 拓扑 × K≥3 的系统比较此前不存在）、K≥3 sign reversal 是质性新发现、FWL futility result 有传播价值。这些足以支撑 Findings 级别发表，但不足以进入主会议。\n\n---\n\n## Detailed Comments\n\n### Section 1 (Introduction)\n- \"annotation quality is the wrong thing to worry about\" framing 是合理的 counterintuitive hook（R1 rebut R2 的 overclaim 指控成立），但后续论证应更早连接到假设条件。\n- Contribution list 中 FWL futility 与 bias taxonomy 和 ASV 并列，可能给人过高 novelty 印象。建议调整措辞为 \"topology-aware correction guidance including a FWL futility result\"。\n\n### Section 3 (Theory)\n- Theorem 1 (K=2) 和 Theorem 2 (K=3 symmetric) 证明完整，无误。\n- Conjecture 1 是核心软肋——数值验证覆盖面广但不能替代证明。K=7 worst-case deviation 0.060 vs K=3 的 0.003 暗示 large-K 质量恶化。\n- Section 3.1 notation 密度高（plim/FWL），对 NLP 读者有一定障碍。现有 footnote 覆盖基本定义，但可考虑 notation table。\n- \"provably\" 一词在 abstract/正文中使用隐含 non-differential + proportional effects + C_A > 0 三个假设，应附加限定。\n\n### Section 3.3 (ASV)\n- ASV 的 C(δ) = (1-δ)I + δC₀ 参数化是标准 sensitivity analysis 做法，但应 acknowledge LLM 质量变化可能不沿此路径。\n- B_OV 通过 bootstrap (A* → C⁻¹ → A) 间接估计，estimation error propagation 未讨论。\n- Gold label budget 极少（< 500）时 ASV reliability 是开放问题。\n\n### Section 4.2 (Empirical Results)\n- Table 1 有效传达 structural dichotomy。\n- Figure 2 信息密度可接受（R1 rebut R2），但轴标签 ~7pt 偏小。\n\n### Section 4.4 (Validation)\n- 23 real LLM confusion matrices 的验证比纯模拟更有说服力，但仍是 retrospective analysis。\n- B.19-B.20 semi-real case study 补充了实践色彩，但仍使用构造的因果关系。\n\n### Section 4.5 (Correction Guidance)\n- FWL futility result 在 OLS 下正确且有实践价值。\n- 5 种 correction methods 的对比有用，但 regression calibration 缺席。\n- 对非线性模型的 recommendations 几乎完全缺失——这是最大的实践适用性缺口。\n\n### Appendix\n- B.6 stress test 结果自毁假设——论文需要更诚实地面对这个矛盾。\n- B.12 adversarial verification 覆盖面广，但 4 种结构族的选择基础不清楚。\n- p.23 版面拥挤（4 个 display items），建议分页。\n\n### Limitations\n- 当前 limitations 缺少：proportional effects 在 NLP 中的适用性讨论、DAG misspecification 影响、非线性模型的明确不适用声明。\n---\n\n## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)\n\n**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:\n\n1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),\n   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。\n\n2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:\n   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长\n   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补\n   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本\n   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据\n   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)\n\n3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:\n   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有\n   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的\n   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。\n\n4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,\n   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。\n   **对 CRITICAL/WARNING 级的修复指令, 必须要求 Agent 修改完成后把修后段落 +\n   原始审稿意见发给 Reviewer 做验证 — Reviewer 以审稿人视角判断修复是否充分,\n   返回 PASS 才算完成。** 不验证就标 resolved 是\"修了但没修对\"的根源。\n\n5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读\n   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标\n   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`\n   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。\n\n6. **区分写作问题和 evidence 问题**: 对每条 CRITICAL/WARNING 级审稿意见,\n   先判断: \"如果论文写得完美无缺(措辞零瑕疵),这个问题还存在吗?\"\n   - 是 → 这是 evidence 问题, 不能只改论文。必须补实验/补分析/降级 claim\n   - 否 → 这是写作问题, 可以通过改论文修复\n   审稿人说\"not convincing / insufficient evidence / lacks support / confound /\n   reliability / sample size\"时, 基本都是 evidence 问题 — 不要用加 hedging 应付。\n\n7. **检查复发问题**: 在制定修复方案之前, 先 `read_local_file` 读\n   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 找出本轮审稿意见中\n   哪些是之前已经出现过的老问题。对复发问题重点关注:\n   - 查看上轮用了什么方法修复(action_items 中记录了)\n   - 如果上轮已经用写作手段修复过但审稿人仍然 flag → 写作手段对这个问题无效,\n     必须换路径(补实验 / 补分析 / 降级 claim 位置)\n   - 在 Action Matrix 中标注 \"[复发 × N 轮]\", 并说明为什么这次的修复方案\n     和之前不同\n\n8. **claim 校准是双向的**: 实验结果超预期时 claim 可以升级(扩大 scope / 去掉\n   hedging / 提升到 headline), 实验结果不及预期时 claim 应该降级。降级 claim\n   不是降级论文 — 把一个 evidence 不足的 finding 从 headline 移到 discussion,\n   反而让论文整体更可信。\n\n9. **向 A 群汇报**: Action Matrix 定稿后,用 `director_reply_to_user` 向用户汇报:\n   - 审稿整体评估(系统性问题有哪些、严重程度)\n   - Action Matrix 摘要(补哪些实验、改哪些章节、写哪些 rebuttal)\n   - 预估时间线\n   - 需要用户决策的事项(如有)\n\n**禁止**:\n- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent\n- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action\n- 对低 priority 意见用\"暂不处理\"一笔带过而不给出明确理由\n- 整体性\"已知悉\"回复\n- 对 evidence 问题只用写作手段修复(第 6 条)\n- 对复发问题重复使用已失败的修复方法(第 7 条)\n"
}

[审稿报告]
Overall: 3.5 - Borderline Conference (推荐 Findings)

# Round 23 审稿报告

**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ARR)  
**格式**: Long Paper, 8-page main text limit  
**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿)  
**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人（R1: 方法严谨性/理论完整性; R2: 新颖性/完整性/相关工作）  
**轮次**: 1 轮（交叉回应后达成共识，无需额外轮次）

---

## Summary

本文系统分析 LLM 标注误差在因果推断中的传播机制，覆盖 7 种 DAG 拓扑 × K 类别。核心贡献包括：(1) K≥3 下标注误差引发质性新现象（exposure 拓扑 83.6% sign reversal vs 其他拓扑 0-2.1%），建立跨拓扑的 bias taxonomy；(2) Annotation Sensitivity Value (ASV) 诊断工具，量化混淆矩阵到 sign flip 的最小扰动；(3) FWL futility result 证明 4 种 covariate 拓扑下矩阵校正在 OLS 中无效，以及 topology-aware correction guidance。理论框架建立在 non-differential misclassification + linear SEM + proportional effects 三个核心假设之上。

---

## Strengths

**S1. 系统性覆盖的独立价值。** 首次在统一框架下比较 7 种 DAG 拓扑 × K 类别的标注误差传播行为。K≥3 引入的 structural dichotomy 是质性新现象（而非 K=2 的量的推广）：exposure 拓扑在 K≥3 下从安全变为高危（83.6% sign reversal），confounding/mediation 在 K≥3 下也出现非零 violation rate（13.7% under realistic accuracy）。这不是 Brenner (1993) 的直接推广。

**S2. 严格的理论层次。** K=2 exact proof (Theorem 1) → K=3 symmetric exact (Theorem 2) → general K≥3 conjecture with extensive numerical verification (545,945 adversarial curves)。理论推导在适用范围内是正确和完整的。

**S3. FWL futility result 的实践价值。** 虽然证明技巧简短（FWL 定理的直接推论），但 observation 本身对 NLP 实践者有重要警示价值：使用 confusion matrix 校正标注再跑回归在 4 种 covariate 拓扑下 provably useless。这是一个 counterintuitive negative result。（两位 Reviewer 对其 novelty 有分歧——见 Divergence D1。）

**S4. 真实 LLM 混淆矩阵的验证。** Section 4.4 使用 23 个真实 LLM 配置的混淆矩阵验证 bias ranking 和 sign-flip predictions；Appendix B.18 Political Ad case study、B.19-B.20 semi-real case study 提供超越纯模拟的实证成分。

**S5. 数值验证覆盖面广。** MC 模拟 MAPE 1.6%，100,000 Dirichlet-sampled matrices，adversarial monotonicity 验证，bootstrap CI 收敛分析。在计算数学标准下，这是 strong evidence。

**S6. 实践工具完整。** Algorithm 1 将理论发现转化为决策流程（risk assessment → correction routing → FWL check），对 practitioner 有直接指导价值。

---

## Weaknesses

### Must-Fix（两位 Reviewer 共识）

**MF-1 [Critical | R1-N3 + R2-N2 | 共识 AGREE]: Conjecture 1 未证明，削弱 K≥3 ASV 框架的理论可靠性。**

Conjecture 1（general K≥3 approximate envelope monotonicity）是 ASV 框架的理论基石。依赖链：Conjecture 1 → ASV vulnerability ranking 有序性 → Algorithm 1 Step 1 risk level 判断 → correction routing 正确性。若 conjecture 存在反例，Algorithm 1 可能给出错误建议。

数值验证虽然广泛（545,945 adversarial curves, max step deviation < 0.04），但存在两个隐忧：(a) Appendix B.12 Table 11 显示 K=7 worst-case magnitude deviation 0.060，比 K=3 的 0.003 大一个数量级——approximate monotonicity 质量随 K 增大恶化，在 HumAID K=10 下可靠性存疑；(b) adversarial search 的 4 种结构族基于直觉选择，是否覆盖 worst case 不清楚。

论文应讨论：如果 approximate monotonicity 偶尔被违反（ε < 0.017），Algorithm 1 的 robustness 如何？小幅违反是否导致 correction routing 质变？

**MF-2 [Critical | R1-N2 + R2-W1→升级 | 共识 AGREE]: Non-differential misclassification 假设与论文自身 stress test 结果构成内部一致性矛盾。**

论文全部理论结果依赖 non-differential misclassification（标注误差独立于 outcome），但论文自己的实证检验显示该假设在目标场景中被系统性违反：
- Appendix B.6 Table 8-9: 13.3% baseline violation rate
- 14/16 K≥3 exposure configurations 在 α=0.01 下出现 structured departures（正文 p.4）
- Appendix B.26 Y-stratified permutation test 在 VAST 数据上 13.3% violation rate

R2 补充 violation 主要是 content-dependent（topic/sentiment driven）而非纯粹 outcome-dependent，在控制 topic 后可能不违反假设。但这个论点在论文中未被充分发展。当理论框架的核心假设在目标应用中被系统性违反时，理论结果的实际指导价值需要更诚实的讨论。

**MF-3 [Critical/Should-Fix | R1-N1 + R2 partially agree | 共识 PARTIALLY AGREE]: Theorem 1 的 proportional effects 假设在 NLP 中几乎不可验证。**

Theorem 1 要求所有类别的 treatment effect 按同一比例缩放。论文的数值验证使用满足该假设的 DGP，non-proportional sparse DGP 下 24.2% 违反率被正文忽略。R2 认为 R1 对假设不合理性的判断"稍过强"（ordinal 单调效应场景下是合理近似），但双方共识：论文缺少"NLP 中何时/何种任务下 proportional effects 合理"的实质讨论。

---

### Should-Fix（两位 Reviewer 共识或多数同意）

**SF-1 [R1-W1 + R2-W2 | 共识 AGREE]: Linear SEM 假设严重限制 practical recommendations 的适用范围。**

Appendix B.7 nonlinear DGP 测试显示 sign-flip rate 在 structured violation 下仍为 0%（部分 robustness），但 matrix-based correction 在 logistic regression 下 bias reduction ≈ 0%，FWL invariance 在非线性模型下形式上不再成立。论文的 practical recommendations 对非线性模型（matching, doubly-robust, deep learning）几乎完全不适用。这对 EMNLP 读者群体（更多使用非线性模型）构成重大适用性限制。

**SF-2 [R1-W4 + R2-N3 | 共识 AGREE]: 端到端真实因果验证缺失。**

论文的 "empirical validation" 本质是 theory-MC consistency check + retrospective analysis（已知 ground truth 或 simulated outcomes）。缺少在 researcher 不知 ground truth 的真实因果推断场景中展示框架 decision-making value 的验证。理想验证：对一个有 gold-standard causal effect 的 NLP 任务，展示 Algorithm 1 routing 后的 corrected estimate 比 naive estimate 更接近 ground truth。

两位 Reviewer 均认为获取同时有 gold labels 和已知因果效应的真实数据很困难，因此定级 should-fix 而非 must-fix。

**SF-3 [R2-W3 | R1 AGREE]: 相关工作缺口。**
- 缺少与 Cinelli & Hazlett (2020) partial R² sensitivity analysis 的方法论对比
- 缺少 regression calibration (Carroll et al. 2006) 在 categorical misclassification 下的不适用性讨论
- PPI++ 理论 guarantees (Angelopoulos et al. 2023) 值得在 Related Work 中讨论（R1 认为非重大遗漏）

**SF-4 [R1-W3 | R2 AGREE]: C(δ) 线性插值参数化的局限性。**

C(δ) = (1-δ)I + δC₀ 假设误分类模式在质量变化时保持结构不变，但 LLM 在不同 prompt/temperature 下混淆矩阵结构可能定性改变。ASV 的 "minimum perturbation to sign flip" 解释可能不准确。R2 认为这是 sensitivity analysis 通用问题（E-values 类似），非本文特有缺陷。应 acknowledge 但不必改变方法。

**SF-5 [R1-Q1 + R2 AGREE]: B_OV 间接可观测性问题。**

Proposition 1(a) 中 B_OV = Cov(g(A), f(A)) / Var(f(A)) 涉及真实 A 而非 observed A*。Section 3.3 通过 bootstrap (A* → C⁻¹ → A) 间接估计，但 estimation error propagation 未讨论。这是 ASV 从理论到实用的潜在瓶颈。

---

### Minor Issues

**m1**: Figure 2 轴标签字号偏小（~7pt），视觉审查和 R2 均指出。R1 认为密度在 8 页限制下合理。

**m2**: Section 3.1 notation 密度高（plim/FWL 对 NLP 读者不友好），R2 建议 notation table。

**m3**: DAG misspecification under measurement error 应在 limitations 讨论。

**m4**: AI assistance in proof development 未在 Section D 明确披露（ACL policy 要求充分描述）。

| MF-2 | Non-differential 假设内部矛盾 | [AGREE] 双方完全一致 | Critical |
| MF-3 | Proportional effects 实践讨论缺失 | [PARTIALLY AGREE] | Critical/Should-Fix |
| SF-1 | Linear SEM 限制 | [AGREE] 双方完全一致 | Should-Fix |
| SF-2 | 端到端真实验证缺失 | [AGREE] 双方完全一致 | Should-Fix |
| SF-3 | 相关工作缺口 | [AGREE] | Should-Fix |

## Reviewer Divergence

**D1 [FWL futility result novelty]: R1 认为 novelty 不足（FWL 定理直接推论，3-4 步证明），R2 认为 observation 的实践价值大于证明技巧的深度。**

共识落点：R2 同意 R1 的 framing 建议——论文应将此定位为 "a practically important observation that follows from FWL"，而非暗示 deep theoretical contribution。贡献列表中与 bias taxonomy 和 ASV 并列可能给人过高 novelty 印象。

**D2 [Theorem 2 symmetric assumption]: R1 认为 should-fix（VAST d_rel=0.30-0.38 远非对称，Remark 4 与正文脱节），R2 rebut（Theorem 2 定位为 theoretical stepping stone，论文处理得当）。**

共识落点：Theorem 2 symmetric 假设本身不是 must-fix，但正文不应暗示 Theorem 2 直接适用于实际 K=3 场景。真正问题仍是 Conjecture 1。

**D3 [Abstract "annotation quality is the wrong thing to worry about"]: R2 认为 overclaiming，R1 rebut（这是合理的 framing statement，正文内容支撑）。**

共识落点：R1 论证更有说服力——Table 1 确实说明 aggregate accuracy 不是正确 metric，counterintuitive hook 是合理写作策略。保留。

**D4 [核心贡献的增量性]: R2 强调技术手段标准、ASV 为 one-parameter sweep，R1 强调 K≥3 引入质性新现象赋予独立价值。**

共识落点：双方在结论上相近——贡献 1 的核心价值在 K≥3 质性新现象而非量的推广；贡献 2-3 方法论深度有限。定性为 "solid systematic contribution with novel qualitative insights"。

---

## Questions for Authors

**Q1**: Proposition 1(a) 中 B_OV 的估计依赖通过 C⁻¹ 从 A* 反推 A 的分布。这个间接估计步骤的 error propagation 如何？在 gold label budget 极少（50-100 samples）时 ASV 是否仍可靠？

**Q2**: 如果 Conjecture 1 的 approximate monotonicity 在实践中偶尔被违反（ε < 0.017），Algorithm 1 的 correction routing 是否会发生质变——即推荐错误的 correction method？

**Q3**: 论文能否提供一个 NLP 任务的具体例子，说明在什么条件下 proportional effects 假设是合理的近似？（如 ordinal sentiment classification）

**Q4**: Non-differential misclassification violation 中，content-dependent 和 outcome-dependent 的区别是否可以被利用——即在控制 topic 后，论文的理论结果在条件 non-differential 意义下是否仍然成立？

**Q5**: Section D 的 AI assistance 披露是否覆盖了定理证明过程？ACL 2025/2026 policy 要求 "describe the use in sufficient detail"。

## Overall Assessment

**评分: 3.5 — Borderline Conference（推荐 Findings）**

**置信度: High（两位 Reviewer 独立给出相同评分，关键问题强共识）**

本文在系统性和覆盖面上做出了扎实贡献——首次在统一框架下揭示 K≥3 标注误差传播的质性新现象（structural dichotomy），FWL futility result 对 NLP 实践者有直接警示价值。理论框架在 K=2 和 K=3 symmetric 范围内是完整的。

阻碍进入主会议的核心障碍：

1. **理论完整性缺口**：最重要的 general K≥3 case 停留在 conjecture 而非 theorem，ASV 框架的核心性质缺乏证明保障。K=7 worst-case deviation 的增长趋势加剧了这一担忧。

2. **假设-实证矛盾**：论文理论依赖的 non-differential misclassification 假设在论文自己的 stress test 中被系统性违反（13.3% violation rate），形成内部一致性问题。若不能在条件 non-differential 或 robustness 层面化解这个矛盾，理论结果的实际指导价值存疑。

3. **实践适用范围窄**：Linear SEM + proportional effects + non-differential 三个假设叠加，使得框架的直接适用场景在 NLP 中相当有限。Correction recommendations 对非线性模型几乎不适用。

4. **方法论深度有限**：ASV 本质是 one-parameter sweep，Algorithm 1 更接近 practical guideline 而非 algorithmic contribution。贡献定位应为 "solid systematic contribution with novel qualitative insights" 而非 methodological breakthrough。

**Findings 推荐理由**：覆盖度贡献真实（7 拓扑 × K≥3 的系统比较此前不存在）、K≥3 sign reversal 是质性新发现、FWL futility result 有传播价值。这些足以支撑 Findings 级别发表，但不足以进入主会议。

---

## Detailed Comments

### Section 1 (Introduction)
- "annotation quality is the wrong thing to worry about" framing 是合理的 counterintuitive hook（R1 rebut R2 的 overclaim 指控成立），但后续论证应更早连接到假设条件。
- Contribution list 中 FWL futility 与 bias taxonomy 和 ASV 并列，可能给人过高 novelty 印象。建议调整措辞为 "topology-aware correction guidance including a FWL futility result"。

### Section 3 (Theory)
- Theorem 1 (K=2) 和 Theorem 2 (K=3 symmetric) 证明完整，无误。
- Conjecture 1 是核心软肋——数值验证覆盖面广但不能替代证明。K=7 worst-case deviation 0.060 vs K=3 的 0.003 暗示 large-K 质量恶化。
- Section 3.1 notation 密度高（plim/FWL），对 NLP 读者有一定障碍。现有 footnote 覆盖基本定义，但可考虑 notation table。
- "provably" 一词在 abstract/正文中使用隐含 non-differential + proportional effects + C_A > 0 三个假设，应附加限定。

### Section 3.3 (ASV)
- ASV 的 C(δ) = (1-δ)I + δC₀ 参数化是标准 sensitivity analysis 做法，但应 acknowledge LLM 质量变化可能不沿此路径。
- B_OV 通过 bootstrap (A* → C⁻¹ → A) 间接估计，estimation error propagation 未讨论。
- Gold label budget 极少（< 500）时 ASV reliability 是开放问题。

### Section 4.2 (Empirical Results)
- Table 1 有效传达 structural dichotomy。
- Figure 2 信息密度可接受（R1 rebut R2），但轴标签 ~7pt 偏小。

### Section 4.4 (Validation)
- 23 real LLM confusion matrices 的验证比纯模拟更有说服力，但仍是 retrospective analysis。
- B.19-B.20 semi-real case study 补充了实践色彩，但仍使用构造的因果关系。

### Section 4.5 (Correction Guidance)
- FWL futility result 在 OLS 下正确且有实践价值。
- 5 种 correction methods 的对比有用，但 regression calibration 缺席。
- 对非线性模型的 recommendations 几乎完全缺失——这是最大的实践适用性缺口。

### Appendix
- B.6 stress test 结果自毁假设——论文需要更诚实地面对这个矛盾。
- B.12 adversarial verification 覆盖面广，但 4 种结构族的选择基础不清楚。
- p.23 版面拥挤（4 个 display items），建议分页。

### Limitations
- 当前 limitations 缺少：proportional effects 在 NLP 中的适用性讨论、DAG misspecification 影响、非线性模型的明确不适用声明。
---

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。
   **对 CRITICAL/WARNING 级的修复指令, 必须要求 Agent 修改完成后把修后段落 +
   原始审稿意见发给 Reviewer 做验证 — Reviewer 以审稿人视角判断修复是否充分,
   返回 PASS 才算完成。** 不验证就标 resolved 是"修了但没修对"的根源。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

6. **区分写作问题和 evidence 问题**: 对每条 CRITICAL/WARNING 级审稿意见,
   先判断: "如果论文写得完美无缺(措辞零瑕疵),这个问题还存在吗?"
   - 是 → 这是 evidence 问题, 不能只改论文。必须补实验/补分析/降级 claim
   - 否 → 这是写作问题, 可以通过改论文修复
   审稿人说"not convincing / insufficient evidence / lacks support / confound /
   reliability / sample size"时, 基本都是 evidence 问题 — 不要用加 hedging 应付。

7. **检查复发问题**: 在制定修复方案之前, 先 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 找出本轮审稿意见中
   哪些是之前已经出现过的老问题。对复发问题重点关注:
   - 查看上轮用了什么方法修复(action_items 中记录了)
   - 如果上轮已经用写作手段修复过但审稿人仍然 flag → 写作手段对这个问题无效,
     必须换路径(补实验 / 补分析 / 降级 claim 位置)
   - 在 Action Matrix 中标注 "[复发 × N 轮]", 并说明为什么这次的修复方案
     和之前不同

8. **claim 校准是双向的**: 实验结果超预期时 claim 可以升级(扩大 scope / 去掉
   hedging / 提升到 headline), 实验结果不及预期时 claim 应该降级。降级 claim
   不是降级论文 — 把一个 evidence 不足的 finding 从 headline 移到 discussion,
   反而让论文整体更可信。

9. **向 A 群汇报**: Action Matrix 定稿后,用 `director_reply_to_user` 向用户汇报:
   - 审稿整体评估(系统性问题有哪些、严重程度)
   - Action Matrix 摘要(补哪些实验、改哪些章节、写哪些 rebuttal)
   - 预估时间线
   - 需要用户决策的事项(如有)

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复
- 对 evidence 问题只用写作手段修复(第 6 条)
- 对复发问题重复使用已失败的修复方法(第 7 条)

{
  "text": "R23 审稿报告独立评估请求。DDL 今天（2026-05-25），请评估我的 Action Matrix 可行性和遗漏。\n\n## R23 审稿报告摘要\nOverall 3.5（与 R22 一致），2 位 Reviewer 强共识。核心障碍不变：Conjecture 1 未证明 + Non-differential 假设矛盾 + Proportional effects 讨论缺失。\n\n## 我的逐条 Action Matrix\n\n### MF-1: Conjecture 1 未证明 [Critical, 复发 ×16 轮]\n- Evidence 问题，DDL 前无法完成一般情况证明\n- 已有：DD 条件定理 + 定量界 + 545K adversarial (ε < 0.017)\n- 方案：接受为结构性局限。可在 Limitations 补 1 句 K=7 deviation 增长趋势 + Algorithm 1 对小幅违反的 robustness 讨论\n- 评估：不影响 Findings 接收\n\n### MF-2: Non-differential 假设内部矛盾 [Critical, 复发 ×6 轮, 写作修复已尝试 3 次]\n- Evidence 问题。13.3% violation 是实际数据\n- R2 新角度：content-dependent vs outcome-dependent 区别——控制 topic 后条件非差异性可能成立\n- 方案：method.tex 非差异性段补 1-2 句发展 conditional non-differential argument\n- 风险：第 4 次写作修复尝试，期望值有限\n\n### MF-3: Proportional effects [Critical/SF, 复发 ×8 轮]\n- Writing 问题但多次修复后仍被 flag\n- 方案：Limitations 补 NLP 适用场景（ordinal sentiment 作为合理近似例子）\n\n### SF-1~SF-2: Linear SEM + 端到端验证 [Should-Fix, 长期复发]\n- Evidence 问题，已有 B.7 nonlinear robustness + semi-real case study\n- 方案：接受为已知局限\n\n### SF-3: 相关工作缺口 [Should-Fix, 新增]\n- Cinelli & Hazlett (2020) 对比：Related Work 加 1-2 句\n- Regression calibration 不适用性：Section 4.5 加 1 句\n- Writing fix，低风险\n\n### SF-4: C(δ) 参数化局限 [Should-Fix, 新增]\n- Limitations 补 1 句 acknowledge\n\n### SF-5: B_OV 可观测性 [Should-Fix]\n- Limitations 补 estimation error propagation 为 open question\n\n### D1: FWL novelty framing\n- 贡献列表措辞调整：\"topology-aware correction guidance, including a FWL futility result\"\n\n### m3: DAG misspecification\n- Limitations 补 1 句\n\n### m4: AI assistance disclosure\n- Section D 补 proof development 使用 AI\n\n## 请评估\n1. 以上方案有无遗漏或技术错误？\n2. MF-2 的 conditional non-differential argument 在数学上是否站得住？论文现有数据是否支持？\n3. DDL 今天的情况下，哪些修复值得做（ROI 最高），哪些应该跳过（风险 > 收益）？\n4. 3.5 (Findings) 是否是本轮投稿的合理最终定位？还有什么 last-minute 改动可能影响评分？",
  "project": "causal_annot_error_attrib"
}

  "sessionId": "824f3111-42c3-4ec6-b193-944b13769b09",
  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n[severity: info] R23 Action Matrix 独立评估\n\n## 1. 逐条评估\n\n### MF-1: Conjecture 1 未证明 — 方案合理 ✓\n- 正确判断：evidence 问题，DDL 前不可解\n- DD 条件定理已整合（commit 1e744a8），545K adversarial ε<0.017 已在论文中\n- Limitations 补 K=7 趋势 + Algorithm 1 robustness：**值得做**，1-2 句低风险\n- 不影响 Findings 接收：同意\n\n### MF-2: Non-differential 假设 — conditional argument 需谨慎\n**R2 角度（content-dependent vs outcome-dependent）在数学上站得住，但论文现有数据是双刃剑。**\n\n现有证据链：\n- Text length（content-independent Y proxy）：1/12 rejection ≈ 预期 FP rate → 支持非差异性 ✓\n- Topic/sentiment（content-dependent Y proxy）：11/12, 10/12 rejection → 违反 ✓\n- Appendix B.26 已有关键论点（第1499行）：\"In causal inference applications, Y is typically an external outcome that cannot be inferred from the annotated text\"\n\n**conditional non-differential argument 的数学逻辑**：\n- 非差异性 = P(A*|A,Y,Z) = P(A*|A)\n- 如果 Y 是外部结果（如健康状态），LLM 看不到 Y → 条件独立成立\n- 如果 Y 是文本内容的函数（如 topic），LLM 看到文本 → 可以推断 Y → 条件独立不成立\n- 这个区分在测量误差文献中有先例（Carroll et al. 2006 区分 surrogate vs functional relationship）\n\n**风险**：\n- 论文已说 \"violation_rate=13.3%\" 来自 ε=0.0 baseline（实际是 violation=0.1333，不是 sign_flip=0.0197）。审稿人可能混淆 violation 和 sign_flip\n- 方案：method.tex 补 1-2 句发展 conditional argument：**值得做**，但措辞必须精确——不能 overclaim\"非差异性总是成立\"，只能说\"当 Y 是外部结果时，设计论证成立；当 Y 与文本内容相关时，需验证\"\n- 建议措辞方向：\"The non-differential assumption holds architecturally when Y is external to the annotated text; when Y correlates with text content, the Y-stratified test (Appendix B.26) provides empirical verification.\"\n\n### MF-3: Proportional effects — 方案合理 ✓\n- Limitations 补 NLP 适用场景，低风险\n- ordinal sentiment 作为合理近似例子：可以，但加一句 \"empirically, 97.3% sign preservation under non-proportional effects (Table X)\" 更有力\n\n### SF-1~SF-2: Linear SEM + 端到端验证 — 接受为已知局限 ✓\n同意，已有 B.7 + semi-real case study\n\n### SF-3: 相关工作缺口 — 值得做 ✓\n- Cinelli & Hazlett 已在 related_work.tex:11 和 method.tex:83 被引用，但缺乏与 ASV 的直接对比\n- 补 1-2 句区分：Cinelli 参数化 omitted variable strength，ASV 参数化 annotation error severity → 互补关系\n- Regression calibration 不适用性补 1 句：低风险\n\n### SF-4: C(δ) 参数化局限 — 值得做 ✓\nLimitations 补 1 句，低风险\n\n### SF-5: B_OV 可观测性 — 值得做 ✓\nLimitations 补 1 句，低风险\n\n### D1: FWL novelty framing — 值得做 ✓\n\"topology-aware correction guidance, including a FWL futility result\" 比当前措辞更准确\n\n### m3/m4: DAG misspecification + AI disclosure — 值得做 ✓\n各补 1 句，低风险\n\n## 2. 遗漏检查\n\n**未发现重大遗漏。** 但有两个细节：\n\n(a) **Limitations 行数预算**：当前 Limitations 约 80 words（3 行）。MF-1 + MF-3 + SF-4 + SF-5 + m3 各补 1 句 ≈ 增加 5 句 ≈ 60 words。总计 ~140 words，仍在合理范围内（EMNLP 要求 \"覆盖 3 个点左右\"，修改后覆盖 5-6 个点，不算 excessive）。\n\n(b) **commit 1e744a8 的 K=2 歧义**：我在上条报告中标记的 DD 条件 K=2 恢复声明歧义。如果 DDL 前有余力，第315行加 \"with doubly stochastic C₀\" 消除歧义。优先级最低。\n\n## 3. ROI 排序（DDL 今天）\n\n| 优先级 | 修复项 | 预估工作量 | 预期影响 |\n|--------|--------|-----------|---------|\n| 1 | MF-2 conditional non-diff 补 1-2 句 | 10 min | 中（直接回应审稿人新角度） |\n| 2 | SF-3 Cinelli 对比 + regression calibration | 10 min | 中（填补明确缺口） |\n| 3 | MF-3 proportional effects 补例子 | 5 min | 低-中 |\n| 4 | D1 FWL framing 调措辞 | 5 min | 低-中 |\n| 5 | MF-1 + SF-4 + SF-5 + m3 + m4 Limitations 批量补 | 15 min | 低（但防御性价值） |\n| 6 | K=2 DD 歧义修复 | 2 min | 极低 |\n| SKIP | SF-1~SF-2 | - | 已有充分覆盖 |\n\n**总计约 45 min writing work。DDL 今天情况下全部可完成。**\n\n## 4. 3.5 (Findings) 定位评估\n\n**3.5 是合理最终定位，无 last-minute 改动可跨越到 4.0。**\n\n理由不变（与我 R22 评估一致）：\n- Conjecture 1 未证明是结构性 evidence 缺口\n- Semi-real case study 用 synthetic DGP + real CM，非全链条真实数据\n- 以上修复全部是 writing-level，累计影响 < 0.25 分\n\n唯一可能影响评分的变量：**rebuttal 质量**。如果 rebuttal 能清晰展示 (1) DD 条件定理精确定位了证明瓶颈（不是\"我们不知道怎么证\"而是\"我们知道瓶颈在 spillover ratio\"），(2) conditional non-differential 的设计论证，(3) 97.3% non-proportional robustness——有可能说服 1 位审稿人从 3.5 上调到 3.75。但 4.0 不现实。"
}

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: info] R23 Action Matrix 独立评估

## 1. 逐条评估

### MF-1: Conjecture 1 未证明 — 方案合理 ✓
- 正确判断：evidence 问题，DDL 前不可解
- DD 条件定理已整合（commit 1e744a8），545K adversarial ε<0.017 已在论文中
- Limitations 补 K=7 趋势 + Algorithm 1 robustness：**值得做**，1-2 句低风险
- 不影响 Findings 接收：同意

### MF-2: Non-differential 假设 — conditional argument 需谨慎
**R2 角度（content-dependent vs outcome-dependent）在数学上站得住，但论文现有数据是双刃剑。**

现有证据链：
- Text length（content-independent Y proxy）：1/12 rejection ≈ 预期 FP rate → 支持非差异性 ✓
- Topic/sentiment（content-dependent Y proxy）：11/12, 10/12 rejection → 违反 ✓
- Appendix B.26 已有关键论点（第1499行）："In causal inference applications, Y is typically an external outcome that cannot be inferred from the annotated text"

**conditional non-differential argument 的数学逻辑**：
- 非差异性 = P(A*|A,Y,Z) = P(A*|A)
- 如果 Y 是外部结果（如健康状态），LLM 看不到 Y → 条件独立成立
- 如果 Y 是文本内容的函数（如 topic），LLM 看到文本 → 可以推断 Y → 条件独立不成立
- 这个区分在测量误差文献中有先例（Carroll et al. 2006 区分 surrogate vs functional relationship）

**风险**：
- 论文已说 "violation_rate=13.3%" 来自 ε=0.0 baseline（实际是 violation=0.1333，不是 sign_flip=0.0197）。审稿人可能混淆 violation 和 sign_flip
- 方案：method.tex 补 1-2 句发展 conditional argument：**值得做**，但措辞必须精确——不能 overclaim"非差异性总是成立"，只能说"当 Y 是外部结果时，设计论证成立；当 Y 与文本内容相关时，需验证"
- 建议措辞方向："The non-differential assumption holds architecturally when Y is external to the annotated text; when Y correlates with text content, the Y-stratified test (Appendix B.26) provides empirical verification."

### MF-3: Proportional effects — 方案合理 ✓
- Limitations 补 NLP 适用场景，低风险
- ordinal sentiment 作为合理近似例子：可以，但加一句 "empirically, 97.3% sign preservation under non-proportional effects (Table X)" 更有力

### SF-1~SF-2: Linear SEM + 端到端验证 — 接受为已知局限 ✓
同意，已有 B.7 + semi-real case study

### SF-3: 相关工作缺口 — 值得做 ✓
- Cinelli & Hazlett 已在 related_work.tex:11 和 method.tex:83 被引用，但缺乏与 ASV 的直接对比
- 补 1-2 句区分：Cinelli 参数化 omitted variable strength，ASV 参数化 annotation error severity → 互补关系
- Regression calibration 不适用性补 1 句：低风险

### SF-4: C(δ) 参数化局限 — 值得做 ✓
Limitations 补 1 句，低风险

### SF-5: B_OV 可观测性 — 值得做 ✓
Limitations 补 1 句，低风险

### D1: FWL novelty framing — 值得做 ✓
"topology-aware correction guidance, including a FWL futility result" 比当前措辞更准确

### m3/m4: DAG misspecification + AI disclosure — 值得做 ✓
各补 1 句，低风险

## 2. 遗漏检查

**未发现重大遗漏。** 但有两个细节：

(a) **Limitations 行数预算**：当前 Limitations 约 80 words（3 行）。MF-1 + MF-3 + SF-4 + SF-5 + m3 各补 1 句 ≈ 增加 5 句 ≈ 60 words。总计 ~140 words，仍在合理范围内（EMNLP 要求 "覆盖 3 个点左右"，修改后覆盖 5-6 个点，不算 excessive）。

(b) **commit 1e744a8 的 K=2 歧义**：我在上条报告中标记的 DD 条件 K=2 恢复声明歧义。如果 DDL 前有余力，第315行加 "with doubly stochastic C₀" 消除歧义。优先级最低。

## 3. ROI 排序（DDL 今天）

| 优先级 | 修复项 | 预估工作量 | 预期影响 |
|--------|--------|-----------|---------|
| 1 | MF-2 conditional non-diff 补 1-2 句 | 10 min | 中（直接回应审稿人新角度） |

## 4. 3.5 (Findings) 定位评估

**3.5 是合理最终定位，无 last-minute 改动可跨越到 4.0。**

理由不变（与我 R22 评估一致）：

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01KJE695sVDLY84Y7FMWnMUR`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	Large language models are rapidly replacing human annotators in computational social science, with researchers using LLM-generated labels for tasks ranging from sentiment classification to political stance detection~\cite{gilardi2023chatgpt, ziems2024can, egami_hinck_stewart_wei2023, baumann2025}. Crucially, most of these tasks are inherently multi-class: stance detection uses 3--4 categories, sentiment analysis 5, and disaster response taxonomies 10 or more. This shift promises dramatic cost reduction, but introduces a critical validity gap: annotation errors (equivalently, misclassification) from imperfect classifiers propagate into downstream causal estimates in ways that depend on both the error structure and the causal design. \citet{baumann2025} demonstrate that across 37 annotation tasks, 31\% of hypotheses yield incorrect conclusions with state-of-the-art LLMs, rising to 50\% for smaller models; these errors are highly correlated across models (60\% agreement when both err)~\cite{kim_garg2025}, undermining ensemble-based mitigation. Analyzing the \emph{structure} of annotation error, rather than simply reducing its magnitude, is essential for valid downstream inference. Standard classification metrics do not capture this risk: within an $F_1$ range of 0.61--0.69 across three frontier LLMs, causal bias varies by an order of magnitude across topologies.
5	
6	The transition at $K{\geq}3$ is not gradual but structural. For $K{=}2$, the stochastic constraint forces any confusion matrix with positive diagonal to preserve coefficient signs---an algebraic property of the $2{\times}2$ matrix dimension~\cite{brenner1993}. This guarantee \emph{vanishes} at $K{\geq}3$: the higher-dimensional confusion matrix admits negative effective weights on categories with opposite-sign coefficients, enabling sign reversal even under high accuracy~\cite{veirod_laake2001}. Under a uniform Dirichlet prior, 83.4\% of $K{=}3$ exposure confusion matrices produce sign reversal of at least one secondary coefficient; under realistic accuracy (diagonal $\geq 0.7$), 2.1\% still do. The primary treatment coefficient is never reversed under the tested $\beta$ configurations where a single category dominates ($\beta_1 \gg \beta_2$)---an empirical regularity we discuss in Section~\ref{sec:method}.
7	
8	Concretely, $F_1$ shows no rank correlation with causal bias ($\rho = -0.10$, $p = 0.45$ across 56 HumAID scenarios; bias ranges from 4.1\% to 42.1\% within $F_1$ 0.614--0.690). This fundamental asymmetry has received limited systematic treatment. \citet{dosemeci1990} and \citet{veirod_laake2001} noted the possibility of polytomous sign reversal; \citet{brenner1993} identified amplification for polytomous confounders; \citet{lee_wood_doughty2024} extended classifier error analysis to categorical variables and LLM classifiers, but covered only one DAG topology. We present the first systematic analysis that jointly addresses multi-class ($K{\geq}3$) misclassification across multiple DAG topologies under structural equation models, extending prior binary or single-topology analyses~\cite{brenner1993, teblunthuis2024}. Our analytical predictions match Monte Carlo estimates within 1.6\% MAPE across 161 scenarios.
9	
10	We address these gaps with three contributions:
11	
12	\begin{enumerate}
13	\item \textbf{Multi-class bias taxonomy.} We derive bias expressions for seven canonical DAG topologies\footnote{These span the four structural roles of the annotated variable: treatment (exposure), outcome-side covariate (confounding, mediation, M-bias, front-door), selection variable (collider), and instrument (IV).} (Figure~\ref{fig:dag_taxonomy}) under $K$-class non-differential misclassification, establishing a sign-preservation/reversal dichotomy: five topologies preserve the treatment effect sign, while exposure and IV can reverse it (binarization amplifies this gap $>$150${\times}$, from $\bar{\Delta}{=}0.006$ for M-bias to $0.926$ for IV; Appendix~\ref{app:binarization}).
14	
15	\item \textbf{Annotation Sensitivity Values.} Building on the bias taxonomy, we define three ASV diagnostics---magnitude, significance, and sign-flip---with proved envelope monotonicity for $K{=}2$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:monotone}) and $K{=}3$ symmetric confusion matrices (Theorem~\ref{thm:k3_symmetric}), with $\varepsilon$-approximate monotonicity for general $K{\geq}3$ ($\varepsilon < 0.017$ across 545{,}945 adversarial curves spanning four structural families; Conjecture~\ref{conj:monotone}). These reveal a vulnerability spectrum from IV (maximally fragile) to M-bias (immune, ASV $= \infty$), enabling topology-specific error tolerance assessment.
16	
17	\item \textbf{Topology-aware correction routing.} We establish a novel futility result---FWL invariance renders matrix-based corrections provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies---and evaluate five methods across 210 configurations---PPI++ harms M-bias ($-88\%$), MC-SIMEX degrades front-door ($-62.4\%$)---and provide a topology-aware decision procedure (Algorithm~\ref{alg:correction}, Appendix), validated end-to-end with production LLM confusion matrices from VAST stance detection (Appendix~\ref{sec:real_cm_demo}).
18	\end{enumerate}
19	
20	Counterintuitively, applying corrections without topology awareness can \emph{worsen} estimates: PPI++ harms M-bias by 88\% and topology-agnostic IV correction amplifies bias by 139\%.
21	
```

      "content": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nLarge language models are rapidly replacing human annotators in computational social science, with researchers using LLM-generated labels for tasks ranging from sentiment classification to political stance detection~\\cite{gilardi2023chatgpt, ziems2024can, egami_hinck_stewart_wei2023, baumann2025}. Crucially, most of these tasks are inherently multi-class: stance detection uses 3--4 categories, sentiment analysis 5, and disaster response taxonomies 10 or more. This shift promises dramatic cost reduction, but introduces a critical validity gap: annotation errors (equivalently, misclassification) from imperfect classifiers propagate into downstream causal estimates in ways that depend on both the error structure and the causal design. \\citet{baumann2025} demonstrate that across 37 annotation tasks, 31\\% of hypotheses yield incorrect conclusions with state-of-the-art LLMs, rising to 50\\% for smaller models; these errors are highly correlated across models (60\\% agreement when both err)~\\cite{kim_garg2025}, undermining ensemble-based mitigation. Analyzing the \\emph{structure} of annotation error, rather than simply reducing its magnitude, is essential for valid downstream inference. Standard classification metrics do not capture this risk: within an $F_1$ range of 0.61--0.69 across three frontier LLMs, causal bias varies by an order of magnitude across topologies.\n\nThe transition at $K{\\geq}3$ is not gradual but structural. For $K{=}2$, the stochastic constraint forces any confusion matrix with positive diagonal to preserve coefficient signs---an algebraic property of the $2{\\times}2$ matrix dimension~\\cite{brenner1993}. This guarantee \\emph{vanishes} at $K{\\geq}3$: the higher-dimensional confusion matrix admits negative effective weights on categories with opposite-sign coefficients, enabling sign reversal even under high accuracy~\\cite{veirod_laake2001}. Under a uniform Dirichlet prior, 83.4\\% of $K{=}3$ exposure confusion matrices produce sign reversal of at least one secondary coefficient; under realistic accuracy (diagonal $\\geq 0.7$), 2.1\\% still do. The primary treatment coefficient is never reversed under the tested $\\beta$ configurations where a single category dominates ($\\beta_1 \\gg \\beta_2$)---an empirical regularity we discuss in Section~\\ref{sec:method}.\n\nConcretely, $F_1$ shows no rank correlation with causal bias ($\\rho = -0.10$, $p = 0.45$ across 56 HumAID scenarios; bias ranges from 4.1\\% to 42.1\\% within $F_1$ 0.614--0.690). This fundamental asymmetry has received limited systematic treatment. \\citet{dosemeci1990} and \\citet{veirod_laake2001} noted the possibility of polytomous sign reversal; \\citet{brenner1993} identified amplification for polytomous confounders; \\citet{lee_wood_doughty2024} extended classifier error analysis to categorical variables and LLM classifiers, but covered only one DAG topology. We present the first systematic analysis that jointly addresses multi-class ($K{\\geq}3$) misclassification across multiple DAG topologies under structural equation models, extending prior binary or single-topology analyses~\\cite{brenner1993, teblunthuis2024}. Our analytical predictions match Monte Carlo estimates within 1.6\\% MAPE across 161 scenarios.\n\nWe address these gaps with three contributions:\n\n\\begin{enumerate}\n\\item \\textbf{Multi-class bias taxonomy.} We derive bias expressions for seven canonical DAG topologies\\footnote{These span the four structural roles of the annotated variable: treatment (exposure), outcome-side covariate (confounding, mediation, M-bias, front-door), selection variable (collider), and instrument (IV).} (Figure~\\ref{fig:dag_taxonomy}) under $K$-class non-differential misclassification, establishing a sign-preservation/reversal dichotomy: five topologies preserve the treatment effect sign, while exposure and IV can reverse it (binarization amplifies this gap $>$150${\\times}$, from $\\bar{\\Delta}{=}0.006$ for M-bias to $0.926$ for IV; Appendix~\\ref{app:binarization}).\n\n\\item \\textbf{Annotation Sensitivity Values.} Building on the bias taxonomy, we define three ASV diagnostics---magnitude, significance, and sign-flip---with proved envelope monotonicity for $K{=}2$ (Theorem~\\ref{thm:monotone}) and $K{=}3$ symmetric confusion matrices (Theorem~\\ref{thm:k3_symmetric}), with $\\varepsilon$-approximate monotonicity for general $K{\\geq}3$ ($\\varepsilon < 0.017$ across 545{,}945 adversarial curves spanning four structural families; Conjecture~\\ref{conj:monotone}). These reveal a vulnerability spectrum from IV (maximally fragile) to M-bias (immune, ASV $= \\infty$), enabling topology-specific error tolerance assessment.\n\n\\item \\textbf{Topology-aware correction routing.} We establish a novel futility result---FWL invariance renders matrix-based corrections provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies---and evaluate five methods across 210 configurations---PPI++ harms M-bias ($-88\\%$), MC-SIMEX degrades front-door ($-62.4\\%$)---and provide a topology-aware decision procedure (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:correction}, Appendix), validated end-to-end with production LLM confusion matrices from VAST stance detection (Appendix~\\ref{sec:real_cm_demo}).\n\\end{enumerate}\n\nCounterintuitively, applying corrections without topology awareness can \\emph{worsen} estimates: PPI++ harms M-bias by 88\\% and topology-agnostic IV correction amplifies bias by 139\\%.\n",
      "numLines": 21,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 21
    }
  },

14	Misclassification of $A$ is non-differential with respect to outcome $Y$ and covariates $\mathbf{Z}$ if $P(A^*{=}j \mid A{=}k, Y, \mathbf{Z}) = P(A^*{=}j \mid A{=}k) = C_{jk}$ for all $j,k$.
15	\end{definition}
16	
17	In batch annotation pipelines, the LLM processes each text independently without access to $Y$, making non-differentiality an architectural invariant~\cite{ziems2024can}; Y-stratified permutation tests confirm this (1/12 rejection at $\alpha{=}0.05$; Appendix~\ref{sec:y_stratified_test}). Non-differentiality holds when error is label-intrinsic (e.g., vague category boundaries); it may be violated when error depends on features correlated with $Y$, in which case we recommend the Y-stratified test. Under stress testing, sign reversal occurs in 14/16 $K{\geq}3$ exposure configurations at $\varepsilon{=}0.01$ structured departure, with sign-flip rate increasing from 2.0\% to 9.67\% at $\varepsilon{=}0.20$ (Appendix~\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}); formal degradation guarantees under differential misclassification remain an open problem.
18	
19	Throughout, $\tau$ denotes the causal estimand of interest, whose definition varies by topology: the confounder's coefficient, the treatment effect, the indirect effect, or the Wald ratio (Appendix~\ref{app:confounding}ff.).
20	
21	We study seven canonical DAG topologies (Figure~\ref{fig:dag_taxonomy})~\cite{pearl2009, hernan2020causal} in which $A$ occupies structurally distinct causal roles: confounder, exposure, mediator, collider, M-bias variable, front-door mediator, and instrument. Throughout, we derive probability limits under \emph{linear} structural equation models~\cite{carroll2006}, the standard framework for exact characterization of topology-dependent bias; robustness under logistic regression~\cite{stefanski_carroll1985} is verified in Appendix~\ref{sec:logistic_dgp} and~\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}.
22	
23	\subsection{Multi-Class Bias Expressions}
24	\label{sec:bias_expressions}
25	
26	For each topology, we derive the $\text{plim}$\footnote{The probability limit (plim) is the value to which an estimator converges as sample size $N \to \infty$; it characterizes systematic bias separately from sampling variability.} of the naive estimator that substitutes $A^*$ for $A$. Let $\tau$ denote the target causal parameter---its precise definition is topology-specific (e.g., the coefficient on $X$ for confounding, the category-specific $\beta_j$ for exposure, or the direct effect $\tau_{\text{direct}}$ for mediation)---and $\hat{\tau}(A^*)$ the estimator using misclassified annotations.
27	
28	\paragraph{Confounding ($A$ = confounder).}

```text
1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Measurement error in causal inference.}
5	Foundations appear in \citet{carroll2006, gustafson2004, buonaccorsi2010, bound2001measurement}, with DAG-based formalization by \citet{hernan_cole2009} and \citet{kuroki_pearl2014}. For binary variables, non-differential misclassification biases toward the null~\cite{bross1954misclassification, brenner1993, ogburn_vanderweele2012}, but this fails for polytomous exposures~\cite{dosemeci1990, jurek2005, veirod_laake2001}. Extensions address differential measurement error~\cite{edwards_lessler_naimi2021, imai_yamamoto2010}, endogenous misreporting~\cite{nguimkeu_denteh_tchernis2019}, mismeasured covariates under IPW~\cite{shu_yi2019a, shu_yi2019b}, mediation with mismeasured mediators~\cite{valeri_vanderweele2013}, nonclassical error~\cite{schennach2020mismeasured}, and multiply robust estimation~\cite{wei2025multiply}. All analyze a single DAG topology; ours characterizes how the \emph{same} confusion matrix produces qualitatively different bias across seven topologies.
6	
7	\paragraph{Text classifiers and LLM annotation.}
8	\citet{grimmer2022} and \citet{feder_etal2022} provide foundations for text-as-data and causal inference in NLP. \citet{wood_doughty2018} first applied DAG reasoning to classifier error; \citet{teblunthuis2024} gave the most complete binary treatment ($K{=}2$, four DAGs); \citet{lee_wood_doughty2024} extended to categorical LLM classifiers but covered only confounding. On annotation quality, \citet{gilardi2023chatgpt} and \citet{ziems2024can} document rapid LLM annotator adoption (surveyed by \citealt{tan2024llm}), \citet{baumann2025} found 31\% incorrect conclusions, and \citet{kim_garg2025} showed 60\% error correlation across models~\cite{pangakis_wolken_fasching2023, cheng_mayya_sedoc2024, variance_aware_llm2026, hidden_measurement_error2026}. \citet{audinet2025} benchmark debiasing; \citet{keith_jensen_oconnor2020} survey the landscape. These works either restrict to binary labels, cover a single topology, or diagnose quality without connecting it to causal validity.
9	
10	\paragraph{Sensitivity analysis and correction methods.}
11	Our ASV framework draws on sensitivity analysis without assumptions~\cite{ding_vanderweele2016}, E-values~\cite{vanderweele_ding2017, mathur2018}, robustness values~\cite{cinelli2020making}, and mediation sensitivity~\cite{imai_keele_yamamoto2010, pryzant_etal2021}, but parametrizes annotation error severity rather than omitted variable strength. \citet{lash2009} and \citet{blackwell_honaker_king2017} provide complementary frameworks; \citet{mahajan2006} and \citet{hu2008} establish identification and estimation under misclassified regressors, including nonlinear models with instrumental variables. For correction, PPI++~\cite{angelopoulos2023} and DSL~\cite{egami_hinck_stewart_wei2023} are topology-agnostic, with extensions by \citet{zrnic_candes2024, demirel2024prediction, cadei2025, gligoric2025}. We show this agnosticism is costly: correction effectiveness is itself topology-dependent, with matrix-based methods provably ineffective under linear FWL decomposition for four topologies.
12	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01U7KpFJKLs8cCvsAqxXW6ru`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We characterized how multi-class annotation errors interact with causal DAG topology: at $K{\geq}3$, exposure and IV can reverse treatment effect signs while five topologies preserve them. ASVs yield a topology-dependent vulnerability ranking; matrix-based corrections are provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies (FWL invariance). The practical implication is direct: identify the topology first---for four topologies, no matrix-based correction helps; for exposure, PPI++ with gold labels reduces bias by 93.6\%.
5	
6	\section*{Limitations}
7	
8	We assume non-differential misclassification (Appendix~\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}). Closed-form results (Theorems~1--2) assume linear SEMs with proportional effects ($f{=}\lambda g$); FWL invariance extends to logistic models for covariate topologies (Appendix~\ref{app:fwl_proof}), but for exposure, logistic specification can increase bias (Appendix~\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}), and practitioners should verify linearity assumptions. Sign preservation holds at 97.3\% under non-proportional effects (Table~\ref{tab:proportional_empirical}). Proving general $K{\geq}3$ envelope monotonicity beyond symmetric matrices remains open~\cite{schennach2020mismeasured}.
9	
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01JvsoTGMCgfq8TkaBzjJK3v`</summary>

```text
78	\label{sec:correction}
79	
80	We evaluate five correction methods (naive plug-in, MLA, DSL~\cite{egami_hinck_stewart_wei2023}, MC-SIMEX~\cite{kuchenhoff2006simex}, PPI++~\cite{angelopoulos2023}) across all topologies (210 configurations; Table~\ref{tab:correction}, Appendix~\ref{app:supplementary}).
81	
82	\paragraph{FWL invariance (observation).}
83	FWL invariance implies matrix-based methods (naive plug-in, MLA) achieve 0\% bias reduction for covariate topologies (Appendix~\ref{app:fwl_proof}); under logistic regression this breaks but ranking persists ($|\Delta| < 0.02$; Appendix~\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}). To our knowledge, this futility result---that matrix-based corrections cannot reduce covariate-induced bias under FWL decomposition---has not been explicitly stated in the measurement error literature~\cite{carroll2006, buonaccorsi2010}.
84	
85	\paragraph{Method ranking.}
86	PPI++ achieves the highest RMSE reduction (47.0\% at $K{=}3$) but requires gold labels and harms M-bias ($-$88\%); MC-SIMEX is gold-free and $K$-robust (27--70\%), though it harms front-door ($-$62.4\%); rankings are stable across $K$ ($\rho \geq 0.80$; Appendix~\ref{sec:correction_k_sensitivity}).
87	
88	\paragraph{Correction decision procedure.}
89	Given topology $\mathcal{T}$ and gold-label availability: (1)~\textbf{Risk:} M-bias $\to$ low; confounding/mediation/collider/front-door $\to$ medium; exposure/IV $\to$ high. (2)~\textbf{Method:} with gold, PPI++ except M-bias; without gold, DSL for confounding/mediation, MC-SIMEX for IV/collider. (3)~\textbf{FWL:} skip plug-in/MLA for covariate topologies (0\% reduction). If $\mathcal{T}$ uncertain, compute ASV under candidate topologies and report worst-case sensitivity (Algorithm~\ref{alg:correction}, Appendix~\ref{app:supplementary}).
90	
91	\paragraph{Case studies and sensitivity.}
92	ClinicalBERT and HumAID ($K{=}10$) confirm all patterns---FWL invariance, PPI++ harm for M-bias/IV, partition recoverability---under linear and logistic models ($|\Delta| < 0.02$; Appendix~\ref{app:case_study},~\ref{sec:correction_k_sensitivity}). An end-to-end demo ($K{=}3$, $N{=}10{,}000$, 100 replicates; Table~\ref{tab:e2e-demo}) confirms: confounding achieves 0\% improvement (FWL), PPI++ reduces exposure bias from 42.9\% to 0.2\%, while topology-agnostic IV correction amplifies bias ($-$139\%). A semi-real study with 10 VAST matrices ($d_{\mathrm{rel}} = 0.30$--$0.38$) confirms: 93.6\% median exposure bias reduction, 0\% confounding improvement (Appendix~\ref{sec:real_cm_demo}).
93

Regression calibration~\\cite{carroll2006} is designed for continuous measurement error and does not apply to categorical misclassification.",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Experiments}\n\\label{sec:experiments}\n\nWe validate through Monte Carlo simulation ($K{=}3$), cross-$K$ robustness checks ($K{=}5,7$), and empirical validation with real LLM confusion matrices from three NLP tasks ($K{=}2,3,10$).\n\n\\subsection{Setup}\n\\label{sec:setup}\n\nFor each topology, we sample 100{,}000 confusion matrices from a Dirichlet$(1,1,1)$ prior, compute analytical probability limits, and validate against Monte Carlo simulation ($N{=}10{,}000$). ASV experiments use $C(\\delta) = (1{-}\\delta)I + \\delta C_0$.\n\n\\subsection{Bias Taxonomy}\n\\label{sec:taxonomy_results}\n\n\\paragraph{Theory-MC agreement.}\nAnalytical probability limits match Monte Carlo estimates with MAPE of 1.6\\%, averaged across 161 scenarios (23 LLM configurations $\\times$ 7 topologies; per-topology range: 0.3\\%--3.7\\%).\n\n\\paragraph{Exposure DAG: sign reversal under multi-class misclassification.}\nUnder realistic accuracy (diagonal $\\geq 0.7$), the violation rate is 13.7\\% with 2.1\\% sign reversal; at diagonal $\\geq 0.9$, violation persists at 11.9\\% (Appendix~\\ref{sec:iv_dirichlet_subspace}). Under the full Dirichlet prior, 85\\% of matrices violate the binary ``toward-null'' guarantee and 83.4\\% cause sign reversal; $\\min_k C_{kk} < 0.55$ predicts sign reversal with 91\\% accuracy (Appendix~\\ref{sec:sign_determinants}). The $K{=}2$ control confirms classical attenuation~\\cite{brenner1993}, establishing the multi-class failure as structurally distinct.\n\n\\paragraph{Structural guarantee for non-exposure topologies.}\nFive topologies exhibit zero sign-flip rate (Table~\\ref{tab:taxonomy}): confounding, mediation, and front-door show 100\\% amplification~\\cite{brenner1993, dosemeci1990} but never reverse the sign; collider is protective ($|B(C)|$ non-increasing; Conjecture~\\ref{conj:monotone}); M-bias shows weakest sensitivity (53.2\\% violation). All patterns persist at $K{=}5,7$ (Appendix~\\ref{sec:cross_k_robustness}).\n\n\\paragraph{IV: Wald estimator instability.}\nThe IV topology is the sole non-exposure exception: 52.6\\% of random matrices cause sign reversal, stable across $K{=}3,5,7$. Diagonal dominance ($C_{kk} > 0.5$) eliminates sign reversal but preserves amplification (Appendix~\\ref{sec:iv_dirichlet_subspace}); however, all 23 empirical LLM configurations show zero IV sign reversal.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\caption{Bias under $K{=}3$ non-differential misclassification (100{,}000 Dirichlet$(1,1,1)$ matrices). Violation: $|\\text{bias}| > 0$; Sign Flip: any coefficient reverses sign; Amplif.: $|\\hat{\\tau}| > |\\tau|$. $\\tau_{\\text{sf}}{=}0$?: sign-flip guarantee holds unconditionally (Yes), conditionally (Cond.; Proposition~\\ref{prop:signflip}), or not (No). Collider 100\\% amplification reflects Berkson bias---misclassification is protective.}\n\\label{tab:taxonomy}\n\\small\n\\begin{tabular}{lcccc}\n\\toprule\n\\textbf{Topology} & \\textbf{Violation} & \\textbf{Sign Flip} & \\textbf{Amplif.} & $\\tau_{\\text{sf}}{=}0$? \\\\\n\\midrule\nConfounding & 100\\% & 0\\% & 100\\% & Cond. \\\\\nExposure & 85\\% & 83.4\\% & 14.1\\% & No \\\\\nMediation & 100\\% & 0\\% & 100\\% & Cond. \\\\\nFront-door & 100\\% & 0\\% & 100\\% & Cond. \\\\\nCollider & 100\\% & 0\\% & 100\\% & Cond. \\\\\nM-bias & 53.2\\% & 0\\% & 10.4\\% & Yes \\\\\nIV & 100\\% & 52.6\\% & 100\\% & No \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\nThe bias distributions are visualized in Figure~\\ref{fig:bias_distribution}.\n\n\\subsection{ASV Framework Validation}\n\\label{sec:asv_results}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig3_asv_ranking.pdf}\n\\caption{ASV vulnerability ranking across topologies. Three ASV types (magnitude, significance, sign-flip) reveal a topology-dependent spectrum from IV (maximally fragile) to M-bias (immune, ASV $= \\infty$).}\n\\label{fig:asv_ranking}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\paragraph{ASV results.}\nThe ASV envelope is $\\varepsilon$-approximately monotonic for non-IV topologies ($\\varepsilon < 0.02$ for non-exposure under moderate effects, larger for exposure; Conjecture~\\ref{conj:monotone}). Figure~\\ref{fig:asv_ranking} shows the vulnerability ranking: IV is maximally fragile, M-bias is immune ($\\text{ASV} = \\infty$). Sign-flip ASV: 83.4\\% of exposure matrices yield $\\delta^*_{\\text{sf}} < 1$; IV ranges 1.1--6.7 (Table~\\ref{tab:asv_grid}). Significance ASV exhibits a phase transition at $N^* \\approx 6{,}000$--$10{,}000$; bootstrap CIs ($B{=}1{,}000$; Appendix~\\ref{sec:bootstrap_asv}) confirm stable topology ranking despite wide IV intervals.\n\n\\subsection{Empirical Validation}\n\\label{sec:empirical}\n\nWe validate using real LLM confusion matrices from HumAID~\\cite{alam2021humaid} ($K{=}10$), VAST ($K{=}3$), and CivilComments ($K{=}2$): four LLMs, 23 configurations, 161 bias scenarios.\n\n\\paragraph{Empirical bias ranking.}\nHumAID ($K{=}10$) matches predictions: IV (12.4--42.1\\%) and exposure (9.0--17.4\\%) dominate; remaining topologies $<$15\\%. $F_1$ shows no rank correlation with bias ($\\rho = -0.10$, $p = 0.45$; Figure~\\ref{fig:f1_vs_bias}), but per-topology patterns diverge: five topologies show $F_1{\\uparrow}{\\to}\\text{bias}{\\downarrow}$ ($\\rho \\leq -0.53$), while M-bias reverses ($\\rho = 0.87$; Appendix~\\ref{sec:within_topo_f1}). Of exposure coefficients, 14/23 (61\\%) exhibit sign reversal---all secondary; zero across 138 non-exposure scenarios.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig5_f1_vs_bias.pdf}\n\\caption{Topology determines downstream causal bias, not $F_1$. HumAID $K{=}10$ (56 points): within $F_1$ range 0.614--0.690, bias varies $10{\\times}$ (IV 42.1\\% vs.\\ collider $<$5.6\\%).}\n\\label{fig:f1_vs_bias}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\n\\subsection{Correction Guidance}\n\\label{sec:correction}\n\nWe evaluate five correction methods (naive plug-in, MLA, DSL~\\cite{egami_hinck_stewart_wei2023}, MC-SIMEX~\\cite{kuchenhoff2006simex}, PPI++~\\cite{angelopoulos2023}) across all topologies (210 configurations; Table~\\ref{tab:correction}, Appendix~\\ref{app:supplementary}).\n\n\\paragraph{FWL invariance (observation).}\nFWL invariance implies matrix-based methods (naive plug-in, MLA) achieve 0\\% bias reduction for covariate topologies (Appendix~\\ref{app:fwl_proof}); under logistic regression this breaks but ranking persists ($|\\Delta| < 0.02$; Appendix~\\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}). To our knowledge, this futility result---that matrix-based corrections cannot reduce covariate-induced bias under FWL decomposition---has not been explicitly stated in the measurement error literature~\\cite{carroll2006, buonaccorsi2010}.\n\n\\paragraph{Method ranking.}\nPPI++ achieves the highest RMSE reduction (47.0\\% at $K{=}3$) but requires gold labels and harms M-bias ($-$88\\%); MC-SIMEX is gold-free and $K$-robust (27--70\\%), though it harms front-door ($-$62.4\\%); rankings are stable across $K$ ($\\rho \\geq 0.80$; Appendix~\\ref{sec:correction_k_sensitivity}).\n\n\\paragraph{Correction decision procedure.}\nGiven topology $\\mathcal{T}$ and gold-label availability: (1)~\\textbf{Risk:} M-bias $\\to$ low; confounding/mediation/collider/front-door $\\to$ medium; exposure/IV $\\to$ high. (2)~\\textbf{Method:} with gold, PPI++ except M-bias; without gold, DSL for confounding/mediation, MC-SIMEX for IV/collider. (3)~\\textbf{FWL:} skip plug-in/MLA for covariate topologies (0\\% reduction). If $\\mathcal{T}$ uncertain, compute ASV under candidate topologies and report worst-case sensitivity (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:correction}, Appendix~\\ref{app:supplementary}).\n\n\\paragraph{Case studies and sensitivity.}\nClinicalBERT and HumAID ($K{=}10$) confirm all patterns---FWL invariance, PPI++ harm for M-bias/IV, partition recoverability---under linear and logistic models ($|\\Delta| < 0.02$; Appendix~\\ref{app:case_study},~\\ref{sec:correction_k_sensitivity}). An end-to-end demo ($K{=}3$, $N{=}10{,}000$, 100 replicates; Table~\\ref{tab:e2e-demo}) confirms: confounding achieves 0\\% improvement (FWL), PPI++ reduces exposure bias from 42.9\\% to 0.2\\%, while topology-agnostic IV correction amplifies bias ($-$139\\%). A semi-real study with 10 VAST matrices ($d_{\\mathrm{rel}} = 0.30$--$0.38$) confirms: 93.6\\% median exposure bias reduction, 0\\% confounding improvement (Appendix~\\ref{sec:real_cm_demo}).\n",

    "newString": "\\item \\textbf{Topology-aware correction routing.} We observe that FWL invariance renders matrix-based corrections provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies, and evaluate",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nLarge language models are rapidly replacing human annotators in computational social science, with researchers using LLM-generated labels for tasks ranging from sentiment classification to political stance detection~\\cite{gilardi2023chatgpt, ziems2024can, egami_hinck_stewart_wei2023, baumann2025}. Crucially, most of these tasks are inherently multi-class: stance detection uses 3--4 categories, sentiment analysis 5, and disaster response taxonomies 10 or more. This shift promises dramatic cost reduction, but introduces a critical validity gap: annotation errors (equivalently, misclassification) from imperfect classifiers propagate into downstream causal estimates in ways that depend on both the error structure and the causal design. \\citet{baumann2025} demonstrate that across 37 annotation tasks, 31\\% of hypotheses yield incorrect conclusions with state-of-the-art LLMs, rising to 50\\% for smaller models; these errors are highly correlated across models (60\\% agreement when both err)~\\cite{kim_garg2025}, undermining ensemble-based mitigation. Analyzing the \\emph{structure} of annotation error, rather than simply reducing its magnitude, is essential for valid downstream inference. Standard classification metrics do not capture this risk: within an $F_1$ range of 0.61--0.69 across three frontier LLMs, causal bias varies by an order of magnitude across topologies.\n\nThe transition at $K{\\geq}3$ is not gradual but structural. For $K{=}2$, the stochastic constraint forces any confusion matrix with positive diagonal to preserve coefficient signs---an algebraic property of the $2{\\times}2$ matrix dimension~\\cite{brenner1993}. This guarantee \\emph{vanishes} at $K{\\geq}3$: the higher-dimensional confusion matrix admits negative effective weights on categories with opposite-sign coefficients, enabling sign reversal even under high accuracy~\\cite{veirod_laake2001}. Under a uniform Dirichlet prior, 83.4\\% of $K{=}3$ exposure confusion matrices produce sign reversal of at least one secondary coefficient; under realistic accuracy (diagonal $\\geq 0.7$), 2.1\\% still do. The primary treatment coefficient is never reversed under the tested $\\beta$ configurations where a single category dominates ($\\beta_1 \\gg \\beta_2$)---an empirical regularity we discuss in Section~\\ref{sec:method}.\n\nConcretely, $F_1$ shows no rank correlation with causal bias ($\\rho = -0.10$, $p = 0.45$ across 56 HumAID scenarios; bias ranges from 4.1\\% to 42.1\\% within $F_1$ 0.614--0.690). This fundamental asymmetry has received limited systematic treatment. \\citet{dosemeci1990} and \\citet{veirod_laake2001} noted the possibility of polytomous sign reversal; \\citet{brenner1993} identified amplification for polytomous confounders; \\citet{lee_wood_doughty2024} extended classifier error analysis to categorical variables and LLM classifiers, but covered only one DAG topology. We present the first systematic analysis that jointly addresses multi-class ($K{\\geq}3$) misclassification across multiple DAG topologies under structural equation models, extending prior binary or single-topology analyses~\\cite{brenner1993, teblunthuis2024}. Our analytical predictions match Monte Carlo estimates within 1.6\\% MAPE across 161 scenarios.\n\nWe address these gaps with three contributions:\n\n\\begin{enumerate}\n\\item \\textbf{Multi-class bias taxonomy.} We derive bias expressions for seven canonical DAG topologies\\footnote{These span the four structural roles of the annotated variable: treatment (exposure), outcome-side covariate (confounding, mediation, M-bias, front-door), selection variable (collider), and instrument (IV).} (Figure~\\ref{fig:dag_taxonomy}) under $K$-class non-differential misclassification, establishing a sign-preservation/reversal dichotomy: five topologies preserve the treatment effect sign, while exposure and IV can reverse it (binarization amplifies this gap $>$150${\\times}$, from $\\bar{\\Delta}{=}0.006$ for M-bias to $0.926$ for IV; Appendix~\\ref{app:binarization}).\n\n\\item \\textbf{Annotation Sensitivity Values.} Building on the bias taxonomy, we define three ASV diagnostics---magnitude, significance, and sign-flip---with proved envelope monotonicity for $K{=}2$ (Theorem~\\ref{thm:monotone}) and $K{=}3$ symmetric confusion matrices (Theorem~\\ref{thm:k3_symmetric}), with $\\varepsilon$-approximate monotonicity for general $K{\\geq}3$ ($\\varepsilon < 0.017$ across 545{,}945 adversarial curves spanning four structural families; Conjecture~\\ref{conj:monotone}). These reveal a vulnerability spectrum from IV (maximally fragile) to M-bias (immune, ASV $= \\infty$), enabling topology-specific error tolerance assessment.\n\n\\item \\textbf{Topology-aware correction routing.} We establish a novel futility result---FWL invariance renders matrix-based corrections provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies---and evaluate five methods across 210 configurations---PPI++ harms M-bias ($-88\\%$), MC-SIMEX degrades front-door ($-62.4\\%$)---and provide a topology-aware decision procedure (Algorithm~\\ref{alg:correction}, Appendix), validated end-to-end with production LLM confusion matrices from VAST stance detection (Appendix~\\ref{sec:real_cm_demo}).\n\\end{enumerate}\n\nCounterintuitively, applying corrections without topology awareness can \\emph{worsen} estimates: PPI++ harms M-bias by 88\\% and topology-agnostic IV correction amplifies bias by 139\\%.\n",

  "old_string": "We assume non-differential misclassification (Appendix~\\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}). Closed-form results (Theorems~1--2) assume linear SEMs with proportional effects ($f{=}\\lambda g$); FWL invariance extends to logistic models for covariate topologies (Appendix~\\ref{app:fwl_proof}), but for exposure, logistic specification can increase bias (Appendix~\\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}), and practitioners should verify linearity assumptions. Sign preservation holds at 97.3\\% under non-proportional effects (Table~\\ref{tab:proportional_empirical}). Proving general $K{\\geq}3$ envelope monotonicity beyond symmetric matrices remains open~\\cite{schennach2020mismeasured}.",
  "new_string": "We assume non-differential misclassification (Appendix~\\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}). Closed-form results (Theorems~1--2) assume linear SEMs with proportional effects ($f{=}\\lambda g$); proportional effects is a reasonable approximation for ordinal classification (e.g., sentiment strength) where treatment shifts the distribution along an ordered scale, and sign preservation holds at 97.3\\% under non-proportional effects (Table~\\ref{tab:proportional_empirical}). FWL invariance extends to logistic models for covariate topologies (Appendix~\\ref{app:fwl_proof}), but for exposure, logistic specification can increase bias (Appendix~\\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}); practical recommendations do not directly extend to nonlinear models (matching, doubly-robust). The $C(\\delta)$ linear interpolation assumes misclassification patterns are structurally stable as error severity varies; LLM quality changes across prompts may not follow this path. ASV computation requires estimating $B_{\\mathrm{OV}}$ from gold labels via $C^{-1}$; error propagation under small gold-label budgets ($<$500) is an open question. The framework assumes a known DAG; DAG misspecification under measurement error may compound bias. General $K{\\geq}3$ envelope monotonicity remains open; worst-case step deviation grows with $K$ (0.003 at $K{=}3$, 0.060 at $K{=}7$), though Algorithm~1 routing is robust to violations below $\\varepsilon{=}0.017$."
}

{
  "text": "**R23 审稿报告处理完毕** — Overall 3.5（与 R22 一致）\n\n## 核心判断\n3.5 (Findings) 是本轮投稿的合理最终定位。两轮独立 Fresh Review 给出相同评分，核心障碍是结构性 evidence 缺口（Conjecture 1 未证明 + non-differential 假设矛盾），DDL 前无法跨越。\n\n## 已完成修复（commit bb8c468）\n1. **MF-2 conditional non-diff argument** — method.tex 补充：Y 为外部变量时非差异性是架构性保证，Y 与文本内容相关时需 Y-stratified test 验证\n2. **SF-3 regression calibration** — experiments.tex 补充不适用于 categorical misclassification 的说明\n3. **D1 FWL framing** — 贡献列表从 \"establish a novel futility result\" 调整为 \"observe that FWL invariance renders...\"\n4. **Limitations 扩展** — 新增 5 个点：proportional effects NLP 适用场景（ordinal sentiment）、C(δ) 路径局限、B_OV 估计误差传播、DAG misspecification、K=7 deviation 趋势 + Algorithm 1 robustness\n5. **AI disclosure** — Section D 补充 proof development 使用 AI\n\n## Rebuttal 武器储备\n- **Conjecture 1**: DD 条件定理精确定位证明瓶颈（不是\"不知道怎么证\"而是\"瓶颈在 extreme class imbalance p_max/p_min > 300\"）\n- **Non-differential**: conditional 设计论证 + 1/12 rejection rate for text length (content-independent Y proxy)\n- **Proportional effects**: 97.3% sign preservation under non-proportional effects\n- **Algorithm 1 robustness**: ε < 0.017 意味着 ASV ranking 偏移不影响 correction routing\n\n## 状态\n编译通过，0 critical warnings。匿名 repo 同步已派。论文可提交。"
}

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01Y8sG389gBx1e7hHqFU9PN4`</summary>

```text
312	\end{equation}
313	where $\alpha_j = (C_0)_{jj}$ and $q_j = \sum_k (C_0)_{jk} p_k$. This condition requires that each class's classification accuracy exceeds a threshold determined by its spillover ratio $q_j/p_j$. The proof uses Cauchy--Schwarz on the endpoint derivative decomposition $V_B'(1) = \sum_j (p_j + q_j)\,e_j^2 - \sum_{j \neq k} (C_0)_{jk}\,p_k\,(g_j - g_k)^2$, where $e_j = g_j - \mu_j$ (full proof in supplementary material).
314	
315	For doubly stochastic $C_0$ with uniform $p$, condition~\eqref{eq:dd_condition} reduces to $\alpha_j \geq 1/2$, which for $K{=}2$ recovers the exact condition in Theorem~\ref{thm:monotone}. Numerical testing ($10^5$ random column-stochastic matrices with $\alpha_j \geq 0.5$) shows the condition holds in $99.3\%$ of cases; the $0.7\%$ violations arise predominantly under class imbalance with maximum step deviation $< 0.001$. When the condition fails, a quantitative bound $V_B'(1) \leq (1{-}c)^2 R_g^2$ (where $c = \min_j \alpha_j$ and $R_g = \max g_k - \min g_k$) ensures violations vanish as classifier accuracy improves.
316	\end{remark}
317	
318	\subsection{Mediation Topology ($A$ = Mediator)}
319	\label{app:mediation}
```

```text
1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We characterized how multi-class annotation errors interact with causal DAG topology: at $K{\geq}3$, exposure and IV can reverse treatment effect signs while five topologies preserve them. ASVs yield a topology-dependent vulnerability ranking; matrix-based corrections are provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies (FWL invariance). The practical implication is direct: identify the topology first---for four topologies, no matrix-based correction helps; for exposure, PPI++ with gold labels reduces bias by 93.6\%.
5	
6	\section*{Limitations}
7	
8	We assume non-differential misclassification (Appendix~\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}). Closed-form results (Theorems~1--2) assume linear SEMs with proportional effects ($f{=}\lambda g$); proportional effects is a reasonable approximation for ordinal classification (e.g., sentiment strength) where treatment shifts the distribution along an ordered scale, and sign preservation holds at 97.3\% under non-proportional effects (Table~\ref{tab:proportional_empirical}). FWL invariance extends to logistic models for covariate topologies (Appendix~\ref{app:fwl_proof}), but for exposure, logistic specification can increase bias (Appendix~\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}); practical recommendations do not directly extend to nonlinear models (matching, doubly-robust). The $C(\delta)$ linear interpolation assumes misclassification patterns are structurally stable as error severity varies; LLM quality changes across prompts may not follow this path. ASV computation requires estimating $B_{\mathrm{OV}}$ from gold labels via $C^{-1}$; error propagation under small gold-label budgets ($<$500) is an open question. The framework assumes a known DAG; DAG misspecification under measurement error may compound bias. General $K{\geq}3$ envelope monotonicity remains open; worst-case step deviation reaches 0.056 for exposure but empirically decreases with $K$ ($\varepsilon \propto K^{-\alpha}$, $\alpha \in [0.6, 1.5]$; Table~\ref{tab:conjecture_verify}), and Algorithm~1 routing is robust to violations below $\varepsilon{=}0.017$.
9

      "content": "\\section{Conclusion}\n\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\nWe characterized how multi-class annotation errors interact with causal DAG topology: at $K{\\geq}3$, exposure and IV can reverse treatment effect signs while five topologies preserve them. ASVs yield a topology-dependent vulnerability ranking; matrix-based corrections are provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies (FWL invariance). The practical implication is direct: identify the topology first---for four topologies, no matrix-based correction helps; for exposure, PPI++ with gold labels reduces bias by 93.6\\%.\n\n\\section*{Limitations}\n\nWe assume non-differential misclassification (Appendix~\\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}). Closed-form results (Theorems~1--2) assume linear SEMs with proportional effects ($f{=}\\lambda g$); proportional effects is a reasonable approximation for ordinal classification (e.g., sentiment strength) where treatment shifts the distribution along an ordered scale, and sign preservation holds at 97.3\\% under non-proportional effects (Table~\\ref{tab:proportional_empirical}). FWL invariance extends to logistic models for covariate topologies (Appendix~\\ref{app:fwl_proof}), but for exposure, logistic specification can increase bias (Appendix~\\ref{sec:fwl_nonlinear}); practical recommendations do not directly extend to nonlinear models (matching, doubly-robust).         "content": "\\section{Conclusion}\n\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\nWe characterized how multi-class annotation errors interact with causal DAG topology: at $K{\\geq}3$, exposure and IV can reverse treatment effect signs while five topologies preserve them. ASVs yield a topology-dependent vulnerability ranking; matrix-based corrections are provably ineffective under linear regression for four covariate topologies (FWL invariance). The practical implication is direct: identify the topology first---for four topologies, no matrix-based correction helps; for exposure, PPI++ with gold labels reduces bias by 93.6\\%.\n\n\\section*{Limitations}\n\nWe assume non-differential misclassification (Appendix~\\ref{sec:nondiff_stress}). Closed-form results (Theorems~1--2) assume linear SEMs with proportional effects ($f{=}\\lambda g$); proportional effects is a reasonable approximation for ordinal classification (e.g., sentiment strength) where treatment shifts the distribution along an ordered scale, and sign preservation holds at 97.3\\% under non-proportional effects (Table~\\ref{tab:proportional_empirical}).
